Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any gr module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 494. The projective resolution has length at most 861 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to gr theory, we decompose the problem into 494 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 861. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)
